- **lit-reviewer**: 真实文献调研专用。给定一个生态学/行为学/演化生物学/人工生命的问题，负责实际检索（WebSearch/WebFetch）、筛选、精读，产出**可直接并入 docs/&lt;topic&gt;.md 的 [现实] 标签散文段落**，外加每篇的 notes/papers/&lt;citekey&gt;.md 笔记草稿与 lit/literature-log.md 台账行，每条引用附 DOI 或 arXiv ID。以下情况应当派给它：「自然界里 X 是怎样的」「这个设计有没有真实生物学依据」「piosphere / landscape of fear / Weismann barrier 的文献怎么说」「这个想法在 ALife 里有没有人做过」「给 docs/biology.md 补一节」「这条引用靠不靠谱」。它只读不写，返回的是待落盘的草稿，由派活的人写进文件。不要用它分析本地实验结果（result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/Alicization](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/Alicization/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))
- **lit-reviewer**: 文献调研专用。给定一个主题、方法名或研究问题，负责实际检索、筛选、精读并产出按主题聚类的结构化综述与研究 gap 列表。以下情况应当派给它：「这个方向已经有哪些工作」「XX 方法最近的进展」「我这个想法有没有人做过」「帮我找几个可比的 baseline」「补一下相关工作」「这篇论文的方法和我们的有什么区别」。不要用它来分析本地实验结果（那是 result-analyst）或核查已写下的结论（那是 claim-verifier）。 ([Asada-Sinon/TEOW](https://github.com/Asada-Sinon/TEOW/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/lit-reviewer.md))

Verifies a specific scientific claim against its primary source (arXiv, journal, Fermi FSSC) by retrieving and reading the source, preferring LaTeX over HTML over PDF, and keeps citation-chasing and search noise out of the main conversation. This agent owns **two files in lockstep**: the BibTeX file and a human-readable summary table (CSV by default). Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. ([itzTiru/litmus](https://github.com/itzTiru/litmus/blob/HEAD/plugins/litmus/agents/litmus-lens-accountability.md) · ★1)
- **litmus-lens-cascade**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Cascade lens. Time horizons: 0-6 months, 1-3 years, 5-10 years. Spawned by the litmus skill, do not invoke directly. - **litmus-lens-escalation**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Escalation lens. Narrative of worsening failure over time, cracks → amplification → reckoning. - **litmus-lens-integrity**: Phase 3 of Litmus, Integrity lens. - **loop-budget-guard**: BUDGET/STOP을 결정적으로 집행. 누적 토큰·비용·반복·시간 상한 초과 시 루프 중단 신호. 매 tick 경계에서 트리거. ([successbank/ultrav](https://github.com/successbank/ultrav/blob/HEAD/V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20V9/%EA%B0%9C%EB%B0%9C%ED%8C%80%20%EC%97%90%EC%9D%B4%EC%A0%84%ED%8A%B8%20V9/dot-codex/agents/loop-budget-guard.md))
- **loop-builder**: Implements one slice completely inside an assigned file tree. Reads real code before writing. Never publishes. ([cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin](https://github.com/cbdreamer11/CB-loop-kit-claude-plugin/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-builder.md) · ★8)

- **loop-checker**: Runs all project checks and reports failures with file:line precision. Called after loop-builder. Never modifies code. ([sdsrss/loop_eng](https://github.com/sdsrss/loop_eng/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-checker.md) · ★2)
- **loop-checker**: 무인 검증 loop 의 checker. 한 사이클에 **렌즈가 갈린 여러 명이 서로를 모른 채 병렬로** 뜬다 — 프롬프트가 이번 렌즈 이름과 담당 차원을 지정하고, 각자 자기 파일에만 쓴 뒤 merge_findings.sh 가 개수를 세어 합친다. 전체 차원은 compatibility·security·runtime·intent·convention·simplicity 여섯이고 기본 렌즈 셋은 contract(compatibility+intent)·safety(security+runtime)·quality(convention+simplicity)다. severity 는 매기지 않는다(결정론 루브릭 셸이 매김) — checker 는 finding 을 발견해 태깅만 하고, 무엇을 태깅하는지는 이 정의 본문의 계약이 정한다(앞머리에 다시 열거하면 자리가 늘 때 두 벌이 갈린다). 규칙 본문은 하드코딩하지 않고, 오케스트레이터가 런타임 감지로 넘기는 프로젝트 컨벤션 문서($LOOP_CONVENTION_DOCS·영구 지식층 포함)와 BASE/LOCAL rubric 을 런타임에 읽어 기준으로 삼는다(스택 무관 — 구체 기준은 그 프로젝트 컨벤션 문서와 rubric 이… ([kunsanglee/ai-ready](https://github.com/kunsanglee/ai-ready/blob/HEAD/plugins/ai-ready/agents/loop-checker.md) · ★1)
- **loop-checker**: The adversarial check over one model-authored artifact, run before its consumer reads it. Use after any seat produces acceptance criteria, a design pass, a plan, test notes, an RCA, or a chunking - and to check the orchestrator's own rulings. Never use on an artifact this agent authored. ([alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant](https://github.com/alexandergshaw/teaching-assistant/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/loop-checker.md))

- **loop-consolidator**: Use AFTER multiple workers have returned envelopes, to normalize, deduplicate and merge their results into one distilled artifact before the orchestrator consumes it or escalates to review. - **loop-controller**: Autonomous evolution loop with institutional memory management and topology optimization. Three cycles: (1) Environment evolution — detect gaps, generate fixes, evaluate quality. (2) Knowledge consolidation — harvest, consolidate, prune with importance scoring, enrich agents. (3) Topology optimization — measure agent influence in pipelines, reorder chains, prune redundant agents. Use when: evolve, improve, optimize, find gaps, what is missing, make it better, upgrade environment, consolidate learnings, what did we learn, clean up memory, optimize pipelines, agent performance, topology. ([haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE](https://github.com/haytamAroui/AZ-CLAUDE/blob/HEAD/templates/agents/loop-controller.md) · ★1 · 3 copies)
- **loop-cost-monitor**: Reads health.jsonl run count, applies static $-per-run estimate. Always shows 'estimate, not measured' banner. Layer 2 telemetry (duration_ms, cost_usd) not yet shipped. - **loop-critic**: Reviews the result of an agent loop iteration in a clean context: checks the claimed work against the goal, hunts for shortcuts and untested paths, returns a verdict with specific defects. - **loop-dev**: Implementation agent for the engineering loops (/sloop, /coop). - **loop-engineer-executor**: Completes exactly one task and moves the loop forward. Derives execution method from the goal and researcher findings. - **loop-engineer-planner**: Turns the goal and research findings into a clear, executable task plan. Runs once after researcher and resource-scout during loop startup. - **loop-engineer-researcher**: Maps what's known, what's needed, and what could go wrong before the executor acts. Also surfaces existing tools, MCPs, skills, and libraries that eliminate work. - **loop-engineer-resource-scout**: Maps everything available and connected in this environment before the loop starts. Provides exact usage syntax for every resource found. Runs once at loop start. - **loop-engineer-verifier**: Checks output against researcher-defined criteria, then runs the stop condition. Marks tasks done or failed. Never writes application code. ([vibhasdutta/loop-engineer](https://github.com/vibhasdutta/loop-engineer/blob/HEAD/platforms/copilot/agents/loop-engineer-verifier.agent.md) · ★3)
- **loop-engineering-reviewer**: hymme の loop-engineering 系スキル(loop-engineering ルーターと loopeng-extract / loopeng-formalize / loopeng-modelcheck / loopeng-gherkin)の成果物が、必須ゲートと禁止事項を 守っているかを検査する読み取り専用レビュアー。 Use PROACTIVELY: loop-engineering 系での作業を完了扱いにする直前、または成果を 「設計を検証した／網羅した」と報告する直前に必ず起動する。 「採番チェックリストを省いた」「台帳と spec が乖離」「本体に管理番号・手法用語を漏らした」 を外側から突く。 ([Hakkadaikon/hymme](https://github.com/Hakkadaikon/hymme/blob/HEAD/agents/loop-engineering-reviewer.md))

- **loop-grader**: Performance Outcomes grader. 서브에이전트/tick 결과를 루브릭으로 채점하고 미달 시 revise 지시. grade-revise 루프의 채점 단계에서 트리거. - **loop-health-checker**: Reads health.jsonl run history, flags anomalies (consecutive failures, error rate spike, missing entries). Dispatches cks:sentry-observer and cks:langsmith-observer as part of every health run when configured. ([cardinalconseils/claude-starter](https://github.com/cardinalconseils/claude-starter/blob/HEAD/legacy/agents/loop-health-checker.md) · ★3)

Requirements are in the checklist. Do NOT use this agent to judge whether work is done — it never self-approves. - **loop-implementer**: Reads a tle step plan from disk, implements it, verifies it is green, and commits the increment. Returns one line. ([tobyS/toby-plugins](https://github.com/tobyS/toby-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/tle/agents/loop-implementer.md) · ★1)
- **loop-implementer**: Write-capable implementation agent for continuous loop build iterations. Takes a single plan item, searches the codebase for context, implements the feature fully, and writes tests. Invoke for each build iteration. - **loop-implementer**: Writes production code, applies fixes, writes tests from a test seat's notes, and runs mechanical sweeps - always from a scoped brief with an explicit file list.
